# Rebuilt civil-war favor system: direct target variables, no selected-value cache.

KYO_cws_clear_selection = {
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_kappa
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tengu
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_eientei
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_hakugyokurou
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_chireiden
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_oni
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_fairies
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tsukumogami
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_moriya
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_senkai
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_magic_forest
}

KYO_cws_select_village = {
	KYO_cws_clear_selection = yes
	set_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village
}

	KYO_cws_clear_selection = yes

KYO_cws_apply_individual_support_ideas = {
	remove_ideas = { KYO_cws_support_tier_1 KYO_cws_support_tier_2 KYO_cws_support_tier_3 KYO_cws_support_tier_4 KYO_cws_support_tier_5 KYO_cws_support_tier_6 KYO_cws_support_village KYO_cws_support_kappa KYO_cws_support_tengu KYO_cws_support_eientei KYO_cws_support_hakugyokurou KYO_cws_support_chireiden KYO_cws_support_oni KYO_cws_support_fairies KYO_cws_support_tsukumogami KYO_cws_support_moriya KYO_cws_support_senkai KYO_cws_support_magic_forest }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_local_support_seats value = 0 }

			has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo
			exists = yes
			has_capitulated = no

				has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_yakumo
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_seija_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_supports_player }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value = 1 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_yakumo
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_yakumo }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_yakumo_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_remilia
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_remilia }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_remilia_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_hijiri
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_hijiri }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hijiri_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_seija
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_seija }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_seija_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_supports_player }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value = 1 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_yakumo
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_yakumo }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_yakumo_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_remilia
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_remilia }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_remilia_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_hijiri
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_hijiri }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hijiri_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_seija
			}
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_seija }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_seija_seats value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_supports_player }
			add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value = 1 }
		}
		KYO_cws_apply_individual_support_ideas = yes
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo
					has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia
					has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri
					has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija
				}
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction }
			}
			KYO_cws_apply_individual_support_ideas = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_individual_ideas_v1_ready
	}
}

KYO_cws_recalculate_support_tier = {
	KYO_cws_sync_support_ideas = yes
}

KYO_cws_add_magic_forest_player_favor_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo }
		add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_yakumo_support value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_yakumo_support value > 5 } }
			set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_yakumo_support value = 5 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia }
		add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_remilia_support value = 1 }

		}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri }
		add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_hijiri_support value = 1 }
		if = {

			set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_hijiri_support value = 5 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija }
		add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_seija_support value = 1 }
		if = {

KYO_cws_clear_legacy_holder = {
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_player
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_yakumo
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_remilia
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_hijiri
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_seija
	every_country = {
		limit = { original_tag = KYO }
		remove_ideas = KYO_cws_yukari_legacy_holder
	}
}

KYO_cws_assign_legacy_holder_player = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder } }
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_player
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_scattered
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_story_active
		add_ideas = KYO_cws_yukari_legacy_holder
		set_variable = { var = KYO_yukari_legacy_progress value = 6 }
	}
}

KYO_cws_assign_legacy_holder_yakumo = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder } }
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_holder_yakumo
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_story_active }
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_story_active
			country_event = { id = KYO_cws.109 days = 1 }
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo exists = yes }
			add_ideas = KYO_cws_yukari_legacy_holder
		}
	}
}

KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_legacy_roll = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_legacy_has_holder } }
		random_list = {
			88 = { }
			12 = {
				add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_legacy_progress_yakumo value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_legacy_progress_yakumo value > 2 } }
					set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_legacy_progress_yakumo value = 3 }
					KYO_cws_assign_legacy_holder_yakumo = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_hakugyokurou
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_chireiden
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_oni
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_fairies
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tsukumogami
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_moriya
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_senkai
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_magic_forest
	set_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tsukumogami
	add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value > 4 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_supports_player
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_supports_player
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
		}
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_remilia_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_hijiri_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_seija_support value = 0 }
	}
	KYO_cws_recalculate_support_tier = yes
}

KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_village }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_kappa }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tengu }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_eientei }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_hakugyokurou }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_chireiden }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_oni }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_fairies }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tsukumogami }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_moriya }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_senkai }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_yakumo }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_magic_forest }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_village
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_kappa
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tengu
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_eientei
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_hakugyokurou
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_chireiden
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_oni
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_fairies
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tsukumogami
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_moriya
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_senkai
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_magic_forest
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_yakumo }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_yakumo }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_village }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_village_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_village = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_kappa_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_kappa = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tengu_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_tengu = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_eientei_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_eientei = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_hakugyokurou = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_chireiden_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_chireiden = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_oni_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_oni = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_fairies_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_fairies = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_tsukumogami = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_moriya = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_senkai_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_senkai = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_yakumo }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_yakumo_last_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_yakumo_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_target_magic_forest = yes
			}
		}
	}
	KYO_cws_enemy_yakumo_legacy_roll = yes
}

			check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_remilia_support value > 4 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_supports_player
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_supports_player
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia
		}
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_remilia_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_hijiri_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_seija_support value = 0 }
	}
	KYO_cws_recalculate_support_tier = yes
}

KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_village }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_kappa }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tengu }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_eientei }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_hakugyokurou }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_chireiden }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_oni }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_fairies }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tsukumogami }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_moriya }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_senkai }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_remilia }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_magic_forest }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_village
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_kappa
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tengu
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_eientei
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_hakugyokurou
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_chireiden
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_oni
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_fairies
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tsukumogami
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_moriya
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_senkai
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_magic_forest
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_remilia }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_remilia }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_village }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_village_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_village = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_kappa_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_kappa = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tengu_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_tengu = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_eientei_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_eientei = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_hakugyokurou = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_chireiden_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_chireiden = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_oni_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_oni = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_fairies_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_fairies = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_tsukumogami = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_moriya = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_senkai_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_senkai = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_remilia }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_remilia_last_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_remilia_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_target_magic_forest = yes
			}
		}
	}
	KYO_cws_enemy_remilia_legacy_roll = yes
}

			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_supports_player
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri
		}
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_yakumo_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_remilia_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_hijiri_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_seija_support value = 0 }
	}

KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_village }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_kappa }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tengu }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_eientei }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_hakugyokurou }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_chireiden }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_oni }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_fairies }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tsukumogami }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_moriya }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_senkai }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_hijiri }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_magic_forest }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_village
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_kappa
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tengu
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_eientei
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_hakugyokurou
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_chireiden
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_oni
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_fairies
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tsukumogami
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_moriya
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_senkai
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_magic_forest
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_hijiri }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_hijiri }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_village }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_village_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_village = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_kappa_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_kappa = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tengu_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_tengu = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_eientei_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_eientei = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_hakugyokurou = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_chireiden_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_chireiden = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_oni_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_oni = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_fairies_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_fairies = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_tsukumogami = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_moriya = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_senkai_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_senkai = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_hijiri }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hijiri_last_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_hijiri_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_target_magic_forest = yes
			}
		}
	}
	KYO_cws_enemy_hijiri_legacy_roll = yes
}

	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_moriya
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_senkai
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_magic_forest
	set_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_moriya
	add_to_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_seija_support value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_seija_support value > 4 }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri
					has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_supports_player
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_yakumo
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_remilia
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_hijiri
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija
			clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_supports_player
			set_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija
		}
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_yakumo_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_remilia_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_hijiri_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_seija_support value = 0 }
	}
	KYO_cws_recalculate_support_tier = yes
}

KYO_cws_enemy_seija_action = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_village }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_kappa }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tengu }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_eientei }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_hakugyokurou }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_chireiden }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_oni }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_fairies }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tsukumogami }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_moriya }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_senkai }
					}
					AND = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_seija }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_magic_forest }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_village
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_kappa
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tengu
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_eientei
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_hakugyokurou
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_chireiden
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_oni
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_fairies
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tsukumogami
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_moriya
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_senkai
		clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_magic_forest
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_seija }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_seija }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_village_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_village }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_village }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_village_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_village = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_kappa_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_kappa }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_kappa_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_kappa = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tengu_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tengu }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tengu_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_tengu = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_eientei_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_eientei }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_eientei_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_eientei = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_hakugyokurou }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_hakugyokurou_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_hakugyokurou = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_chireiden_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_chireiden }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_chireiden_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_chireiden = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_oni_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_oni }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_oni_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_oni = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_fairies_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_fairies }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_fairies_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_fairies = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_tsukumogami }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_tsukumogami_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_tsukumogami = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_moriya_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_moriya }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_moriya_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_moriya = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_senkai_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_senkai }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_senkai_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_senkai = yes
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_magic_forest_owner_seija }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_seija_last_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 3 has_country_flag = KYO_cws_selected_magic_forest }
				modifier = { factor = 2 check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_magic_forest_seija_support value > 2 } }
				KYO_cws_enemy_seija_target_magic_forest = yes
			}
		}
	}
	KYO_cws_enemy_seija_legacy_roll = yes
}

#############################################
# 河童：1940年代中型坦克設計
#############################################

KYO_cws_grant_kappa_tank_design = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "No Step Back"
		}

		# 直接取得中期中型坦克底盤
		set_technology = {
			improved_medium_tank_chassis = 1
		}

		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "Kappa Type-40 'Kawagiri'"
			type = medium_tank_chassis_2
			parent_version = 0

			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = tank_medium_cannon
				turret_type_slot = tank_medium_two_man_tank_turret
				suspension_type_slot = tank_bogie_suspension
				armor_type_slot = tank_welded_armor
				engine_type_slot = tank_gasoline_engine

				special_type_slot_1 = tank_radio_2
				special_type_slot_2 = tank_sloped_armor
			}
		}

		# 河童直接協助建立生產線
		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = {
				type = medium_tank_chassis_2
				creator = "KYO"
				version_name = "Kappa Type-40 'Kawagiri'"
			}

			requested_factories = 4
			progress = 0.35
			amount = 150
		}
	}

	# 沒有NSB時直接送中期中型坦克
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = medium_tank_equipment_2
			amount = 120
		}

		army_experience = 15
	}
}


#############################################
# 天狗：1940年代戰鬥機設計
#############################################

KYO_cws_grant_tengu_fighter_design = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "By Blood Alone"
		}

		set_technology = {
			improved_small_airframe = 1
		}

		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "Tengu Type-40 'Hayate'"
			type = small_plane_airframe_2

			modules = {
				fixed_main_weapon_slot = aircraft_cannon_1_2x
				fixed_auxiliary_weapon_slot_1 = heavy_mg_2x

				engine_type_slot = engine_3_1x

				special_type_slot_1 = self_sealing_fuel_tanks_small
				special_type_slot_2 = fuel_tanks_small
			}
		}

		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = {
				type = small_plane_airframe_2
				creator = "KYO"
				version_name = "Tengu Type-40 'Hayate'"
			}

			requested_factories = 4
			progress = 0.35
			amount = 150
		}
	}

	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = fighter_equipment_2
			amount = 120
		}

		air_experience = 15
	}
}

#############################################
# 畜生界
# 特色：機動、突擊、燃料、裝甲部隊
#############################################

KYO_cws_grant_beast_focus_reward = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_beast_reward_claimed
			}
		}

		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_beast_secured
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_beast_reward_claimed

		add_manpower = 18000
		army_experience = 20
		add_fuel = 6000

		# 基本武器
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1500
		}

		# 畜生界機動軍
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment
			amount = 500
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = 150
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = anti_tank_equipment
			amount = 100
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = armored_support_vehicle
			amount = 120
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 150
		}

		capital_scope = {
			add_resource = {
				type = oil
				amount = 3
			}

			add_resource = {
				type = rubber
				amount = 2
			}

			add_resource = {
				type = steel
				amount = 2
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			has_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_beast_support_applied
			}
		}

		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_beast_support_applied

		KYO_cws_add_magic_forest_player_favor_1 = yes
	}
}


#############################################
# 河童
# 特色：坦克、工程、車輛、支援裝備
#############################################

KYO_cws_grant_kappa_focus_reward = {

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_kappa_reward_claimed
			}
		}

		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_kappa_secured
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_kappa_reward_claimed

		army_experience = 20
		add_fuel = 4500

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1000
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 600
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = motorized_equipment
			amount = 400
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = 120
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = anti_air_equipment
			amount = 100
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment
			amount = 30
		}

		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = armored_support_vehicle
			amount = 100
		}

		# 河童提供真正有價值的中期坦克設計
		KYO_cws_grant_kappa_tank_design = yes

		capital_scope = {

			add_resource = {
				type = steel
				amount = 4
			}

			add_resource = {
				type = tungsten
				amount = 3
			}

			add_resource = {
				type = chromium
				amount = 2
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			has_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_kappa_support_applied
			}
		}

		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_focus_kappa_support_applied

		KYO_cws_add_tengu_player_favor_1 = yes
	}
}

KYO_cws_grant_last_chance_reward = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_last_chance_reward_claimed }
		}
		KYO_cws_recalculate_support_tier = yes
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_last_chance_reward_claimed

		# 0-2 support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > -1 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 3 }
			}
			add_manpower = 5000
			add_political_power = 15
			add_fuel = 1000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 500 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 30 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_1 days = 45 }
		}

		# 3-4 support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > 2 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 5 }
			}
			add_manpower = 10000
			add_political_power = 25
			army_experience = 5
			add_fuel = 1500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 750 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 150 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 50 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 40 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 1 }
				add_resource = { type = rubber amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_2 days = 60 }
		}

		# 5-7 support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > 4 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 8 }
			}
			add_manpower = 15000
			add_political_power = 35
			army_experience = 10
			air_experience = 5
			add_fuel = 2500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1000 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 200 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 75 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 75 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = 30 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = small_plane_airframe_0 amount = 25 }
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = fighter_equipment_0 amount = 25 }
			}
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 2 }
				add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_3 days = 60 }
		}

		# 8-9 support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > 7 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 10 }
			}
			add_manpower = 22500
			add_political_power = 45
			army_experience = 10
			air_experience = 10
			add_fuel = 3500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1250 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 250 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 100 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_tank_equipment amount = 40 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = small_plane_airframe_0 amount = 50 }
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = fighter_equipment_0 amount = 50 }
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane_equipment amount = 3 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 2 }
				add_resource = { type = tungsten amount = 1 }
				add_resource = { type = rubber amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_4 days = 75 }
		}

		# 10-11 support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > 9 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 12 }
			}
			add_manpower = 30000
			add_political_power = 60
			army_experience = 15
			air_experience = 15
			add_command_power = 10
			add_fuel = 5000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1500 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 300 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 125 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 140 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = 50 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_tank_equipment amount = 50 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = small_plane_airframe_0 amount = 75 }
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = fighter_equipment_0 amount = 75 }
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane_equipment amount = 5 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 3 }
				add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = 2 }
				add_resource = { type = tungsten amount = 1 }
				add_resource = { type = rubber amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_5 days = 75 }
		}

		# 12 or more support seats
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value > 11 }
				check_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_seats value < 100 }
			}
			add_manpower = 45000
			add_political_power = 80
			army_experience = 20
			air_experience = 20
			add_command_power = 20
			add_fuel = 7500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2000 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 400 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 150 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 200 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_air_equipment amount = 75 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = anti_tank_equipment amount = 75 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dlc = "By Blood Alone" }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = small_plane_airframe_0 amount = 120 }
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = fighter_equipment_0 amount = 120 }
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = transport_plane_equipment amount = 8 }
			capital_scope = {
				add_resource = { type = steel amount = 4 }
				add_resource = { type = aluminium amount = 3 }
				add_resource = { type = tungsten amount = 2 }
				add_resource = { type = rubber amount = 2 }
				add_resource = { type = oil amount = 1 }
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = KYO_cws_final_offensive_6 days = 90 }
		}
	}
}


# 八雲：裝備精良的山地特種部隊
KYO_cws_create_prewar_yakumo_package = {
	set_technology = { tech_mountaineers = 1 }
	
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2500 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 450 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 300 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 150 }
	division_template = {
		name = "Boundary Special Operations Division"
		division_names_group = JAP_INF_01
		is_locked = no
		force_allow_recruiting = no
		priority = 2
		regiments = {
			mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
			mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 2 }
			mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 0 }
			mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			mountaineers = { x = 2 y = 0 }
			mountaineers = { x = 2 y = 1 }
			mountaineers = { x = 2 y = 2 }
			artillery_brigade = { x = 3 y = 0 }
			artillery_brigade = { x = 3 y = 1 }
			artillery_brigade = { x = 3 y = 2 }
		}
		support = {
			engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
			recon = { x = 0 y = 1 }
			artillery = { x = 0 y = 2 }
			logistics_company = { x = 0 y = 3 }
			signal_company = { x = 0 y = 4 }
		}
	}
	capital_scope = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Boundary Special Operations Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.85 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			count = 5
		}
	}
}

KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_yakumo_package = {
	add_manpower = 15000
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 750 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 150 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 100 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 50 }
	capital_scope = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Boundary Special Operations Division\" start_experience_factor = 0.90 start_equipment_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			count = 1
		}
	}
}

		regiments = {

			motorized = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			motorized = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			motorized = { x = 2 y = 0 }

KYO_cws_apply_prewar_power_packages = {
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_yakumo exists = yes }
		KYO_cws_create_prewar_yakumo_package = yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
		KYO_cws_create_prewar_remilia_package = yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri exists = yes }
		KYO_cws_create_prewar_hijiri_package = yes
	}
	every_country = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_seija exists = yes }
		KYO_cws_create_prewar_seija_package = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 0 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 1 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 2 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 3 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 4 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 5 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 6 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 7 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 8 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 9 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_remilia_package = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 3 } }
		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			add_ideas = KYO_cws_prewar_remilia_1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value > 5 } }

			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_remilia exists = yes }
			remove_ideas = KYO_cws_prewar_remilia_1
			add_ideas = KYO_cws_prewar_remilia_2
		}
	}
	
	if = {

		every_country = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = KYO_civil_role_hijiri exists = yes }
			KYO_cws_reinforce_prewar_hijiri_package = yes
		}

KYO_cws_debug_fast_forward_prewar = {
	set_country_flag = KYO_cws_debug_prewar_fast_forward_used
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = JAP }
		annex_country = { target = JAP transfer_troops = no }
	}
	set_country_flag = youkai_win_war
	set_country_flag = youkai_japan_war
	set_country_flag = yukari_will_not_survive
	set_country_flag = KYO_renko_plan_b_activated
	set_global_flag = youkai_japan
	clr_global_flag = KYO_four_way_civil_war_over
	clr_global_flag = KYO_four_way_civil_war_active
	clr_country_flag = KYO_civil_war_unified
	clr_country_flag = KYO_four_way_civil_war_started
	clr_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active
	set_variable = { var = KYO_fulfillment_military_committee value = 50 }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_fulfillment_faith_committee value = 50 }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_fulfillment_mobilization_committee value = 50 }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_remilia_power_var value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_hijiri_power_var value = 2 }
	set_variable = { var = KYO_civil_war_seija_power_var value = 2 }
	set_country_flag = KYO_war_leader_remilia
	set_country_flag = KYO_order_leader_byakuren
	set_country_flag = KYO_network_leader_sekibanki
	KYO_snapshot_hyakki_for_civil_war = yes
	set_stability = 0.50
	set_war_support = 0.50
	add_political_power = 500
	add_manpower = 200000
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 5000 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 1000 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = 500 }
	country_event = kyo_postwar.11
}

KYO_cws_initialize_system = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = KYO_player_civil_war_faction
			is_ai = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active }
		}
		set_country_flag = KYO_cws_system_active
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_support_tier value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_legacy_progress_player value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = KYO_cws_legacy_progress_yakumo value = 0 }
